		// 表单日期组件语言：laydate 仅内置 cn/en，扩展 es/th；datetimepicker 默认识别 zh-cn/en/es/th
		var LAYDATE_LANG = (LANG_COUNTRY === 'zh_CN' || lang === 'zh') ? 'cn'
				: (LANG_COUNTRY === 'es_MX' || lang === 'es') ? 'es'
				: (LANG_COUNTRY === 'th_TH' || lang === 'th') ? 'th'
				: 'en';
		var DATETIMEPICKER_LANG = (LANG_COUNTRY === 'zh_CN' || lang === 'zh') ? 'zh-cn'
				: (LANG_COUNTRY === 'es_MX' || lang === 'es') ? 'es'
				: (LANG_COUNTRY === 'th_TH' || lang === 'th') ? 'th'

		//下发商户国家当前墙钟串，算出与本机时间的毫秒差，供已打补丁的 laydate 读取（默认 0=不改，无上下文时≈0）。

			if (__m) {
				window.__laydateNowOffsetMs__ = new Date(+__m[1], +__m[2] - 1, +__m[3], +__m[4], +__m[5], +__m[6]).getTime() - Date.now();
			}
		} catch (e) {}

		function i18n(msgKey) {
			try {
				var msg = $.i18n.prop(msgKey);
				//员工 Line 文案里的聊天工具名按当前国家展示（与后端 CountryAwareMessageSource 同一白名单）
				if (IM_NAME && IM_NAME !== 'Line' && typeof msgKey === 'string' && typeof msg === 'string'
						&& (msgKey.indexOf('offadmin.profile.line') === 0
							|| msgKey.indexOf('offadmin.user.employeeLine') === 0
							|| msgKey.indexOf('offadmin.msg.offlineAddEmployeeLine') === 0)) {
					msg = msg.replace(/\bLine\b/g, IM_NAME);
				}
				return msg;
			} catch (e) {
				console.error(e);
				return msgKey;
			}

	<script>
		// 按后台当前语言覆盖表格组件内置提示，仅影响分页、工具栏和空数据等展示文案。
		(function () {
			function formatMessage(messageKey, values) {
				var message = i18n(messageKey);
				$.each(values || [], function (index, value) {
					message = message.replace(new RegExp('\\{' + index + '\\}', 'g'), value);
				});
				return message;
			}

			$.extend($.fn.bootstrapTable.defaults, {
				formatLoadingMessage: function () {
					return i18n('ui.bootstrapTable.loading');
				},
				formatRecordsPerPage: function (pageNumber) {
					return formatMessage('ui.bootstrapTable.recordsPerPage', [pageNumber]);
				},
				formatShowingRows: function (pageFrom, pageTo, totalRows, totalNotFiltered) {
					if (totalNotFiltered !== undefined && totalNotFiltered > totalRows) {
						return formatMessage('ui.bootstrapTable.showingFilteredRows',
								[pageFrom, pageTo, totalRows, totalNotFiltered]);
					}
					return formatMessage('ui.bootstrapTable.showingRows', [pageFrom, pageTo, totalRows]);
				},
				formatSRPaginationPreText: function () {
					return i18n('ui.bootstrapTable.previousPage');
				},
				formatSRPaginationPageText: function (page) {
					return formatMessage('ui.bootstrapTable.page', [page]);
				},
				formatSRPaginationNextText: function () {
					return i18n('ui.bootstrapTable.nextPage');
				},
				formatDetailPagination: function (totalRows) {
					return formatMessage('ui.bootstrapTable.detailPagination', [totalRows]);
				},
				formatClearSearch: function () {
					return i18n('ui.bootstrapTable.clearSearch');
				},
				formatSearch: function () {
					return i18n('ui.bootstrapTable.search');
				},
				formatNoMatches: function () {
					return i18n('ui.bootstrapTable.noMatches');
				},
				formatPaginationSwitch: function () {
					return i18n('ui.bootstrapTable.paginationSwitch');
				},
				formatPaginationSwitchDown: function () {
					return i18n('ui.bootstrapTable.paginationShow');
				},
				formatPaginationSwitchUp: function () {
					return i18n('ui.bootstrapTable.paginationHide');
				},
				formatRefresh: function () {
					return i18n('ui.bootstrapTable.refresh');
				},
				formatToggle: function () {
					return i18n('ui.bootstrapTable.toggle');
				},
				formatToggleOn: function () {
					return i18n('ui.bootstrapTable.cardViewShow');
				},
				formatToggleOff: function () {
					return i18n('ui.bootstrapTable.cardViewHide');
				},
				formatColumns: function () {
					return i18n('ui.bootstrapTable.columns');
				},
				formatColumnsToggleAll: function () {
					return i18n('ui.bootstrapTable.toggleAll');
				},
				formatFullscreen: function () {
					return i18n('ui.bootstrapTable.fullscreen');
				},
				formatAllRows: function () {
					return i18n('ui.bootstrapTable.allRows');
				},
				formatAutoRefresh: function () {
					return i18n('ui.bootstrapTable.autoRefresh');
				},
				formatExport: function () {
					return i18n('ui.bootstrapTable.export');
				},
				formatJumpTo: function () {
					return i18n('ui.bootstrapTable.jumpTo');
				},
				formatAdvancedSearch: function () {
					return i18n('ui.bootstrapTable.advancedSearch');
				},
				formatAdvancedCloseButton: function () {
					return i18n('ui.bootstrapTable.close');
				},
				formatFilterControlSwitch: function () {
					return i18n('ui.bootstrapTable.filterSwitch');
				},
				formatFilterControlSwitchHide: function () {
					return i18n('ui.bootstrapTable.filterHide');
				},
				formatFilterControlSwitchShow: function () {
					return i18n('ui.bootstrapTable.filterShow');
				}
			});
		})();
	</script>

		(function () {
			if (!window.layui || typeof LAYDATE_LANG === 'undefined') {
				return;
			}
			layui.use('laydate', function () {
				layui.laydate.set({ lang: LAYDATE_LANG });
			});
		})();

                    title : i18n('loan.contractList.Contract.manual.modify.date'),
                    visible:false
                }]
        };
        $.table.init(options);

        // if (!isPlatForm) {
        //     var merchantIdList = new Array();
        //     $("#merchantIdList option:selected").each(function () {
        //         merchantIdList.push($(this).val());
        //     });
        //     $.table.merchantAndApplyCascade(merchantIdList,$("#srcChnTeamList"));
        //     $.table.switchDeptByMerchant(merchantIdList,$("#deptIdCodeList"),"premind")
        // }
        //设置商户应用
        $("#merchantIdList").change(function () {

            var merchantIdList = new Array();
            $("#merchantIdList option:selected").each(function () {
                merchantIdList.push($(this).val());
            });
            $.table.merchantAndApplyCascade(merchantIdList,$("#srcChnTeamList"));
            //$.table.switchDeptByMerchant(merchantIdList,$("#deptIdCodeList"),"premind")
        });

        if(disabledFlag){
            $("#merchantIdList").val(merchantList[0].deptId).trigger('change');
        }
    });

    function onEditableSave (field, row,rowIndex, oldValue, $el) {
        var data = {};
        data.contractId=row.contractId;
        data.contractStatus = row[field];
        data.uncollectible = row.uncollectible;
        if("uncollectible"==field){
            $.operate.saveNoFlush(table.options.updateUncollectibleUrl,data,$el);
        }else{
            $.operate.saveNoFlush(table.options.updateContractStatusUrl,data,$el);
        }
    }
    function valueFormatter(value) {
        if ($.common.isNotEmpty(value))
            return value;
        else
            return '';
    }


    //合同状态跳转
    function contractStateJump() {
        var contractIds = $.table.selectColumns("contractId");
        if($.common.isEmpty(contractIds)){
            $.modal.alertWarning(i18n("loan.common.Contract.batch.modify.tips"));
            return false;
        }
        var url = prefix + "/contractStateJump/" + contractIds;
        $.modal.open(i18n("loan.common.Contract.batch.modify.status"),url);
    }

    //批量修改可摧
    function contractCollectibleJump() {
        var contractIds = $.table.selectColumns("contractId");
        if($.common.isEmpty(contractIds)){
            $.modal.alertWarning(i18n("loan.common.Contract.batch.modify.tips"));
            return false;
        }
        var url = prefix + "/batchContractUncollectible/" + contractIds;
        $.modal.open(i18n("loan.common.Contract.batch.modify.collectable"),url);
    }

    //撤销抹账
    function cancleAccount() {
        var url = prefix +"/cancleAccount";
        $.modal.open(i18n("loan.common.Contract.Cancel.account"),url,"",300);
    }

    //人工悔贷
    function artificialRegretLoan(){
        var contractIds = $.table.selectColumns("contractId");
        if(contractIds == ''){
            $.modal.alertWarning(i18n("common.selectedOne"));
            return false;
        }
        table.set();
        $.modal.confirm(i18n("loan.contractList.contract.confirmRegretLoan"), function() {
            $.modal.loading(i18n("common.progressData"));
            $.post(table.options.regretLoanUrl, {"contractIds":contractIds.toString()}, function(result) {
                if (result.code == web_status.SUCCESS) {
                    $.modal.alertSuccess(result.msg)
                } else if (result.code == web_status.WARNING) {
                    $.modal.alertWarning(result.msg)
                } else {
                    $.modal.alertError(result.msg);
                }
                $.modal.closeLoading();
            });
        });
    }


    function openTable(title, url, width, height){
        //如果是移动端，就使用自适应大小弹窗
        if ($.common.isMobile()) {
            width = 'auto';
            height = 'auto';
        }
        if ($.common.isEmpty(title)) {
            title = false;
        }
        if ($.common.isEmpty(url)) {
            url = "/404.html";
        }
        if ($.common.isEmpty(width)) {
            width = 800;
        }
        if ($.common.isEmpty(height)) {
            height = ($(window).height() - 50);
        }
        layer.open({
            type: 2,
            area: [width + 'px', height + 'px'],
            fix: false,
            //不固定
            maxmin: true,
            shade: 0.3,
            title: title,
            content: url,
            btn: [i18n("loan.common.close")],
            // 弹层外区域关闭
            shadeClose: true,
            yes: function (index, layero) {
                layer.close(index);
            },
            cancel: function(index) {
                return true;
            }
        });
    }

    function searchList(type) {
        if ("1" == type){
            $("#searchType").val("1");
        } else{
            $("#searchType").val("");
        }
        $.table.search();
    }
</script>
</body>
</html>
